Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the stellar structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 559. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 919 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_559(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 559. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^919(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Each subproblem has a solution by the stability criterion fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
